style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Introductory Note</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">This Current Report on Form 8-K is being filed in connection with the completion (the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Closing</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;) on July 31, 2026 by Liberty Global Holding B.V. (&#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Ziggo Group</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221; or the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Company</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;) of the acquisition from Vodafone Europe B.V. (&#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Vodafone</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;) and Vodafone International 1 S.&#224; r.l. (together, the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Vodafone Sellers</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;), subsidiaries of Vodafone Group Plc, of (i) all of the issued and outstanding shares held by Vodafone in VodafoneZiggo Group Holding B.V. (&#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">VodafoneZiggo</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;), representing 50% of the issued share capital of VodafoneZiggo, and (ii) all of the shareholder loans held by Vodafone International 1 S.&#224; r.l. in VodafoneZiggo Group B.V. (collectively, the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Transaction</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;). The Transaction was completed pursuant to the Sale and Purchase Agreement, dated February 18, 2026 (the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Sale and Purchase Agreement</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;), by and among the Vodafone Sellers, the Company and Liberty Global Broadband I Limited, a subsidiary of Liberty Global Ltd. (&#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Liberty Global</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;) that was previously disclosed on the Current Report on Form 8-K of Liberty Global filed on February 24, 2026. Following a series of reorganization transactions, the Company owns, directly or indirectly, all of the equity interests in Telenet Group Holding NV/SA (&#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Telenet</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;) and VodafoneZiggo group. With respect to the 66.8% in Wyre Holding BV owned directly or indirectly by Telenet, the Company owns, directly or indirectly, a 50% minus one share of the economic interest of Telenet&#8217;s interest in Wyre Holding BV (with the remaining 50% plus one share of the economic interest being for the benefit of Liberty Global only). As previously disclosed, Liberty Global intends to distribute its entire equity interest in Ziggo Group to Liberty Global&#8217;s shareholders and list the shares of Ziggo Group on the Euronext Amsterdam stock exchange (the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Spin Transaction</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;).</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:115%">Item 1.01 ENTRY INTO A MATERIAL DEFINITIVE AGREEMENT</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Shareholders&#8217; Agreement </span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The information provided in the Introductory Note of this Current Report on Form 8-K is incorporated by reference into this Item 1.01.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">On July 31, 2026, in connection with the completion of the Transaction, the Company, a subsidiary of Liberty Global, Liberty Global Broadband I Limited and Vodafone entered into a shareholders&#8217; agreement relating to the Company (the &#8220;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:120%">Shareholders&#8217; Agreement</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8221;), pursuant to which the parties established the Company&#8217;s post-Closing governance framework and addressed related shareholder rights and obligations, including those summarized below. </span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The Shareholders&#8217; Agreement provides that, if the Spin Transaction has not occurred eighteen months following the effective date of the Transaction, Vodafone will be entitled to appoint one director to the Company&#8217;s supervisory board. The Shareholders&#8217; Agreement also provides Vodafone with minority investor protections, including consent rights over related party transactions and certain actions of the Company affecting Vodafone&#8217;s rights or economic interests and information rights, and the right to require the Company to make certain distributions if specified conditions are met. </span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">In addition, the Shareholders&#8217; Agreement contains customary restrictions on transfers of Company shares, including a pre-listing lock-in period applicable to certain transfers, rights of first offer, tag-along and drag-along rights, and provisions relating to sales, listings and winding up of the Company.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The foregoing description of the Shareholders&#8217; Agreement does not purport to be complete and is qualified in its entirety by reference to the full text of the Shareholders&#8217; Agreement, a copy of which is filed as Exhibit 10.1 to this Current Report on Form 8-K and is incorporated herein by reference.</span></div><div><span><br/></span></div><div><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:115%">Item 2.01 COMPLETION OF ACQUISITION OR DISPOSITION OF ASSETS</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The information provided in the Introductory Note of this Current Report on Form 8-K is incorporated by reference into this Item 2.01.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">The aggregate consideration payable to the Vodafone Sellers under the Sale and Purchase Agreement consisted of:</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">&#8364;1.0 billion in cash, subject to customary locked-box adjustments for leakage; and</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-18pt"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">&#8226;</span><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%;padding-left:14.5pt">the issuance by the Company to Vodafone at Closing of Class B ordinary shares representing 10% of the issued share capital of the Company.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Following completion of the Transaction, the Company owns 100% of the issued share capital of the VodafoneZiggo group, and Vodafone holds a minority equity interest in the Company.</span></div><div style="height:42.75pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div><span><br/></span></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"/><div style="min-height:42.75pt;width:100%"><div><span><br/></span></div></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">In connection with the completion of the Transaction, the existing shareholders&#8217; agreement governing VodafoneZiggo was terminated. In addition, the Company and its shareholders entered into the Shareholders&#8217; Agreement described under Item 1.01 above.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Times New Roman',serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:120%">Certain agreements, including new and continuing service agreements, between VodafoneZiggo, Vodafone and Liberty Global have been put in place further to the terms agreed in the context of the Transaction, and the Company group is furthermore expected to enter into certain services agreements with entities of the Liberty Global group.</span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span><br/></span></div><div style="text-align:justify"><span style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Supervisory Board, </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">and each person appointed, a </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Supervisory Director</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">)&#59; and</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%">(b)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:115%;padding-left:20.69pt">in the event that no Listing has occurred by the date that is eighteen (18) months following the Effective Date, the Company shall have a Board and a Supervisory Board including the Vodafone Director appointed in accordance with Clause 1.5.</font></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(each person appointed as a member of the Board or the Supervisory &#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;Board, a </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Director</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">).</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.2</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">In the event of Clause 1.1(b), each of LG and the Company shall promptly take all steps, including to amend the Articles (and, in the case of LG, to vote in favour of any resolution of the General Meeting), necessary to give effect to&#58; (a) the creation of the Supervisory Board (in addition to the Board)&#59; and (b) a direct appointment right of Vodafone to appoint the Vodafone Director, in each case, with effect from the date that is eighteen (18) months following the Effective Date.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Duties and responsibilities</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.3</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The Board will be responsible for managing the Company and its business. The Supervisory Board shall, if established, be responsible for supervising the Board.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.4</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The General Meeting shall appoint, and may suspend and&#47;or dismiss, any Director, subject to Clause 1.5. If established, LG shall nominate one of the Supervisory Directors as the chair of the Supervisory Board.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Vodafone Director</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.5</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">In the event of Clause 1.1(b), Vodafone shall be entitled, by notice in writing to the Company and the other Shareholders in accordance with Clause 23, to appoint one (1) Supervisory Director from time to time (the </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Vodafone Director</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">). Any individual appointed as the Vodafone Director shall be in accordance with the requirements set out in Clause 4.1(A) of the ISP.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.6</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The Vodafone Director shall not be suspended, removed or dismissed from office by any Shareholder (other than Vodafone) or the Company other than at the request of Vodafone. </font></div><div style="height:90pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:7pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">4</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.7</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The Shareholders and the Company shall promptly take such steps as may be necessary, including by voting in favour of such appointment pursuant to any resolution of the General Meeting, to effect&#58; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(a)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.92pt">the appointment of any individual nominated or appointed by Vodafone as the Vodafone Director&#59; and</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(b)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.69pt">the suspension, dismissal or removal from office of any individual as the Vodafone Director upon Vodafone serving a notice upon the Company requesting such suspension, dismissal or removal in accordance with Clause 23. </font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Meetings of the Board</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.8</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The members of the Board shall have the discretion to determine the arrangements for and the convening of meetings</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:140%"> </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">of the Board, subject to the Articles and applicable Law.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Convening Supervisory Board meetings</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.9</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">Any Supervisory Director may call a meeting of the Supervisory Board.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If a Vodafone Director has been appointed in accordance with Clause 1.5, the Company shall ensure that the Supervisory Board holds meetings at least once every three (3) months (unless Vodafone agrees otherwise). </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If a Vodafone Director has been appointed in accordance with Clause 1.5, such Supervisory Director shall receive all information about the business and affairs of the Group reasonably necessary to properly perform its duties as a Supervisory Director.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.12</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If a Supervisory Director is not able to attend a Supervisory Board meeting, that Supervisory Director may give a written proxy to another Supervisory Director to vote in accordance with their instructions at such Supervisory Board meeting on their behalf.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Notice of Supervisory Board meetings</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.13</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">At least three (3) Business Days&#8217; notice of each meeting of the Supervisory Board shall be given to each Supervisory Director entitled to attend and the notice shall be accompanied by an agenda setting out in such reasonable detail as may be practicable in the circumstances the subject matter of the meeting. A Supervisory Board meeting may be held at shorter notice than set out in in the preceding sentence or without notice, if the prior written consent (including over email) of all Supervisory Directors has been obtained.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.14</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If a Vodafone Director has been appointed in accordance with Clause 1.5, the Company shall, subject to Clause 1.13&#58; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(a)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.92pt">notify the Vodafone Director of all meetings of the Supervisory Board at the same time and in the same manner as the other Supervisory Directors&#59; and</font></div><div style="height:90pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:7pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">5</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.69pt">provide the Vodafone Director with a copy of all information provided to the other Supervisory Directors at the same time, in the same form and in the same manner as it is provided to them.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Quorum at Supervisory Board meetings</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.15</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">Subject to Clause 1.16, a quorum shall exist at any Supervisory Board meeting if at least one Supervisory Director is present.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.16</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If a Vodafone Director has been appointed in accordance with Clause 1.5&#58; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(a)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.92pt">a quorum shall exist at any Supervisory Board meeting only if&#58; (i) at least two (2) Supervisory Directors are present (or at least one (1) Supervisory Director is present and has received a written proxy from another Supervisory Director in accordance with Clause 1.12)&#59; and (ii) the Vodafone Director is present (or has provided a written proxy to another Supervisory Director in accordance with Clause 1.12)&#59; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(b)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.69pt">if a quorum is not present at a meeting of the Supervisory Board at the time when any business is considered, any Supervisory Director may require that the meeting be reconvened and at least four (4) Business Days&#8217; notice of the reconvened meeting shall be given unless all the Supervisory Directors agree to a shorter period&#59; and </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(c)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:21.72pt">at the reconvened meeting, a quorum shall exist with respect to those matters on the agenda which were not disposed of at the original meeting if at least one Supervisory Director other than the Vodafone Director is present (or has provided a written proxy to another Supervisory Director in accordance with Clause 1.12).  </font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Voting at Supervisory Board meetings</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.17</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">Resolutions of the Supervisory Directors shall be decided by a majority of the votes cast and each Supervisory Director shall have one vote. In the case of an equality of votes, the chair of the meeting shall have a casting vote.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Participation arrangements of the Supervisory Board</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.18</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">To the extent permitted by the Articles and applicable Law, any one or more Supervisory Directors may participate in and vote at Supervisory Board meetings by means of a conference telephone or any communication equipment which allows all persons participating in the meeting to communicate to the others any information or opinions they have on any particular item of business of the meeting. Any Supervisory Director so participating in a meeting shall be deemed to be present in person and shall count towards the quorum.</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:5.2pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;position:relative;top:-2.8pt;vertical-align:baseline"> </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.19</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">Proceedings of Supervisory Board meetings and related papers, minutes and notices shall be in English.</font></div><div style="height:90pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:7pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">6</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Written resolution of the Supervisory Board</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.20</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">A resolution of the Supervisory Directors may be in writing and without convening a Supervisory Board meeting, provided that all Supervisory Directors have consented in writing (including over email) to adopting a resolution outside a meeting. This resolution, if passed, shall be as valid and effective for all purposes as a resolution passed by the Supervisory Directors at a Supervisory Board meeting duly convened, held and constituted.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Supervisory Board Committees</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.21</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">Subject to the requirements of applicable Law, the Supervisory Board shall not have any independent committees of the Board. All decisions for the Supervisory Board shall be brought before the entire Supervisory Board.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Directors&#8217; and officers&#8217; insurance</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.22</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">The Company shall purchase and maintain directors&#8217; and officers&#8217; insurance for each Director. Such insurance shall be purchased and maintained on market standard terms which comply with applicable Law and cover those liabilities which are customary for a company whose business activities are similar to those undertaken by the Group and otherwise shall be subject to customary terms and conditions.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Directors&#8217; remuneration and expenses</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.23</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">No Director shall be entitled to receive any remuneration from the Company (or any Group Member) by way of salary, commission, fees or otherwise in relation to the performance of their duties as a Director, provided that Directors will be entitled to reimbursement by the Company or another Group Member of all reasonable, properly incurred and documented out-of-pocket expenses (plus any applicable Taxes) incurred by them in connection with their office as Directors.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Related Party Agreements </font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">1.24</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">LG shall procure that no Group Member shall take or effect any Related Party Agreement Action, without the prior written consent of Vodafone (such consent not to be unreasonably withheld or delayed, provided that such approval will be deemed to have been given unless Vodafone style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">The delivery of a ROFO Offer Notice by LG shall constitute an irrevocable binding commitment by LG (and&#47;or any of its nominated Affiliate(s)) (as applicable) to purchase the ROFO Shares at a price per Share equal to the ROFO Floor Price on and subject to the ROFO Offer Terms.</font></div><div style="height:90pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">ROFO offer rejected</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">If the ROFO Shareholder rejects the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice (which it shall be entitled to do in its absolute discretion), it shall notify LG of such rejection in writing within ten (10) Business Days of receipt of a compliant ROFO Offer Notice, provided that if the ROFO Shareholder has not served a notice rejecting or accepting the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice by such date, it shall be deemed to have rejected the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice and the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice shall be deemed to have expired.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font the ROFO Offer Notice, the ROFO Shareholder shall be free to&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.92pt">for a period of five (5) months thereafter, enter into a binding agreement for the Transfer of the ROFO Shares to a third party (</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Third Party Agreement</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">), provided that&#58; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:108pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font per cent higher than the ROFO Floor Price included in the ROFO Offer Notice&#59; </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:108pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(ii)</font><font favourable to the third party than the ROFO Offer Terms&#59; and </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:108pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font and</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font paragraph (a), complete the Transfer of the ROFO Shares to such third party. Such completion shall take place no later than twelve (12) months from the date of execution of the Third Party Agreement.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">9.6</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">If the ROFO Shareholder (i) fails to enter into a Third Party Agreement within the five (5) month period specified in Clause 9.5(a), or (ii) having entered into a Third Party Agreement, does not complete the Transfer of the ROFO Shares under the Third Party Agreement within the twelve (12) month period specified in Clause 9.5(b), then the ROFO Shareholder shall not be entitled to serve another ROFO Notice for a period of twelve (12) months from the expiry of the relevant period in which such failure occurred, being the five (5) month period in (i) or the twelve (12) month period in (ii), as applicable.</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">ROFO offer accepted</font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">9.7</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.67pt">If the ROFO Shareholder accepts the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice, it shall notify LG of such acceptance in writing within fifteen (15) Business Days of receipt of the ROFO Offer Notice (failing which the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice shall expire). The ROFO Shareholder and LG (and&#47;or its nominated Affiliate(s)) shall complete the sale and purchase of the ROFO </font></div><div style="height:90pt;position:relative;width:100%"><div style="bottom:0;position:absolute;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:7pt;font-weight:400;line-height:232%">24</font></div></div></div><hr style="page-break-after:always"><div style="min-height:72pt;width:100%"><div style="text-align:right"><font><br></font></div></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">Shares on the terms set out in the ROFO Offer Notice (unless agreed otherwise between LG and the ROFO Shareholder). Completion of the sale and purchase of the ROFO Shares (unless agreed otherwise between LG and the ROFO Shareholder) shall take place on the date which is the later of&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font the offer in the ROFO Offer Notice&#59; and</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font been satisfied or waived,</font></div><div style="padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify"><font sale(s)).</font></div><div><font><br></font></div><div style="text-indent:36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:700;line-height:100%">Indirect Days after receipt of such offer. </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font each Tag Shareholder shall sell a Tag Proportion of its Shares to the Tag Purchaser on the terms set out in the Tag Along Notice (unless otherwise agreed between the Tag Shareholder and the Tag Purchaser) at the same time as and conditional upon the completion of the Tag Sale. If a Tag Shareholder rejects a Tag Along Notice or has not accepted it within the above-mentioned twenty (20) Business Day period, such Tag Shareholder shall be deemed to have declined the offer set out in the Tag Along Notice, and accordingly such offer shall be deemed to have lapsed. LG shall then be free to Transfer its Shares to the Tag Purchaser.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font agreement and a Dutch law governed power of attorney (the </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Tag</font><font The Tag Documents shall be signed and made available to LG within such ten (10) Business Day period, but the Tag PoA shall be held to such Tag Shareholder&#8217;s order until, and shall be deemed automatically released when, the consideration payable to such Tag Shareholder in accordance with this Clause 10 has been paid in cash in accordance with this Clause 10 and Clause 17. </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">10.7</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">If LG, or any entity directly or indirectly Controlling LG, proposes an indirect Transfer of any Shares to a third party (</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Indirect Sale</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">), each Tag Shareholder shall have the right to require LG (and&#47;or its nominated Affiliate(s)) to purchase a Tag Proportion of the Shares held by such Tag Shareholder at the relevant time. This purchase by LG (and&#47;or its nominated Affiliate(s)) shall be on terms economically equivalent to those applicable to the corresponding indirect Interest in the Company subject to the Indirect Sale. The sale and purchase of each Tag Shareholder&#8217;s Shares by LG (and&#47;or its nominated Affiliate(s)) pursuant to this Clause 10.7 shall be conditional upon the completion of the Indirect Sale. Clause 10 shall apply&#160;</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">mutatis mutandis</font><font style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:108pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(iii)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:18.7pt">if the Higher Number is not more than &#91;***&#93; per cent of the Lower Number, the Cash Equivalent Value, as the case may be, will be the arithmetic average of such two numbers&#59;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:108pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(iv)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:18.27pt">if the Higher Number is more than &#91;***&#93; per cent of the Lower Number, the Cash Equivalent Value, as the case may be, will be&#58;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:144pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(A)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.09pt">the Higher Number, if the value determined by the Banker (the </font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-weight:700;line-height:140%">Third Number</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">) is greater than the Higher Number&#59;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:144pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(B)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:20.07pt">the Lower Number, if the Third Number is lower than the Lower Number&#59;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:144pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(C)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.94pt">the arithmetic average of the Third Number and the other number (Higher Number or Lower Number) that is closer to the Third Number, if the Third Number falls within the range between (and including) the Lower Number and the Higher Number&#59; or</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:144pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">(D)</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:19.22pt">the Third Number, if the Lower Number and the Higher Number are equally close to the Third Number.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">13.2</font><font enter into, as soon as reasonably practicable, terms of appointment with the Bankers&#59;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font arbitrator&#59;</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font If any such provision is held to be or becomes invalid or unenforceable under the Law of any jurisdiction, the Parties shall use all reasonable efforts to replace it </font></div><div any question regarding its existence, validity or termination, shall be referred to and finally resolved by arbitration under the Rules of Arbitration of the International Chamber of Commerce, which Rules are deemed to be incorporated by reference into this Clause. </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%">33.2</font><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">The tribunal shall consist of three arbitrators. The parties to the Dispute shall each nominate one arbitrator, provided that where there are multiple claimants or multiple respondents, the multiple claimants jointly and the multiple respondents jointly shall nominate an arbitrator. The third arbitrator, who shall be the presiding arbitrator on the tribunal, shall be nominated by agreement of the parties to the Dispute or, if the parties fail to agree on a nomination within twenty (20)&#160;Business Days of the nomination date of the second arbitrator, the third arbitrator shall be selected and appointed by the ICC Court.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font style="color:#000000;font-family:'Verdana',sans-serif;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;line-height:140%;padding-left:13.32pt">The seat, or legal place, of the arbitration shall be London, the United public domain, save and to the extent that disclosure is required pursuant to a legal duty, to protect or pursue a legal right, or to enforce or challenge an award in legal proceedings before a state court or other legal authority.</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font if a Party applies to any competent court for the commencement of winding up, administration or other insolvency proceedings in respect of the other Party on the basis of an alleged debt claim arising out of or in connection with this Agreement, that court shall have jurisdiction to make a ruling pertaining to the merits of the debt claim if and to the extent required </font></div><div are deemed to be incorporated by reference into this Clause. </font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font The parties to the Dispute shall each nominate one arbitrator, provided that where there are multiple claimants or multiple respondents, the multiple claimants jointly and the multiple respondents jointly shall nominate an arbitrator. The third arbitrator, who shall be the presiding arbitrator on the tribunal, shall be nominated by agreement of the parties to the Dispute or, if the parties fail to agree on a nomination within twenty (20)&#160;Business Days of the nomination date of the second arbitrator, the third arbitrator shall be selected and appointed by the ICC Court.</font></div><div and</font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:72pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font recipient of the Payment and that other person are, taken together, in the same position as that in which they would have been had the matter giving rise to the Payment Obligation not occurred), provided that the amount of the Payment shall not exceed that which it would have been if it had been regarded for all Tax purposes as received solely by the recipient and not any other person.</font></div><div style="text-align:justify"><font><br></font></div><div style="margin-bottom:9pt;padding-left:36pt;text-align:justify;text-indent:-36pt"><font
